prefix-set LABEL
  10.20.30.2/32 le 32,
  20.32.0.0/24 le 32,
  202.123.10.0/24 le 32,
  190.123.0.0/16 le 32,
  116.119.0.0/16 le 32,
  100.73.0.0/16 le 32
end-set
!
prefix-set switch-mgmt
end-set
!
community-set CS-T1
  #-remark match circle T1 Loopbacks
  ios-regex '1113:.*'
end-set
!
community-set CS-T2
  #-remark match circle T1 Loopbacks
  ios-regex '1213:.*'
end-set
!
community-set CS-T3
  #-remark match circle T1 Loopbacks
  ios-regex '1313:.*'
end-set
!
community-set COMM-T5
  1513:9999
end-set
!
community-set CS-ALL-T4
  #-remark match T4 Loopbacks
  ios-regex '14..:.*'
end-set
!
community-set CS-ALL-T5
  #-remark match T5 Loopbacks
  ios-regex '15..:.*'
end-set
!
community-set CS-ALL-T6
  #-remark match T6 Loopbacks
  ios-regex '16..:.*'
end-set
!
route-policy CSR
  if destination in PFX_CSR then
    pass
  elseif community matches-any CS-T1 or community matches-any CS-T2 or community matches-any CS-T3 then
    pass
  elseif community matches-any (8889:8889, 8888:8888) then
    pass
  else
    drop
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy NONE
  drop
end-policy
!
route-policy PASS
  pass
end-policy
!
route-policy pass
  pass
end-policy
!
route-policy LABEL
  if destination in LABEL then
    pass
  else
    drop
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy SID011
  set label-index 011
  set community (1204:011)
  set community (1204:9999) additive
end-policy
!
route-policy SID0111
  set label-index 0111
  set community (1204:0111)
  set community (1204:9999) additive
  set community (1204:1111) additive
end-policy
!
route-policy set-wlp
  set weight 0
  set local-preference 0
end-policy
!
route-policy SID040512
  set community (1411:0512)
  set community (1411:9999)
  set label-index 40512
end-policy
!
route-policy SID060307
  set community (1312:0307)
  set community (1312:9999)
  set label-index 60307
  set aigp-metric igp-cost
end-policy
!
route-policy SID101211
  set label-index 1211
  set community (1204:1211)
  set community (1204:9999) additive
end-policy
!
route-policy SID10040507
  set community (1312:0507)
  set community (1312:9999)
  set label-index 40507
end-policy

  if destination in switch-mgmt then
    pass
  endif
end-policy

route-policy SID500-30515
  set label-index 30515
  set community (1511:0515)
  set community (1511:9999)
  set aigp-metric igp-cost
end-policy
!
route-policy BLOCK-DEFAULT
  if destination in (0.0.0.0/0) then
    drop
  else
    set next-hop self
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy RP-SET-WEIGHT
  if community matches-any CS-ALL-T5 or community matches-any CS-ALL-T6 then
    set weight 100
  else
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy nexthop_not_bgp
  if not protocol is bgp 9730 then
    pass
  else
    drop
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy BLOCK-DEFAULT-V6
  if destination in (::/0) then
    drop
  else
    set next-hop self
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy Loopback100_drop
  if community matches-any (1312:0507) then
    drop
  else
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy add-path-to-ibgp
  set path-selection backup 1 install
  set path-selection all advertise
end-policy
!
route-policy Loopback10X_50X_drop
  if community matches-any (1411:0512) then
    drop
  else
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy add-path-to-ibgp-vrf
  set path-selection backup 1 install
end-policy
!
route-policy RPL_BHJH_DROP_REST_T4_T5
  #-remark Drop other circle T4 & T5 Loopbacks, Replace Circle-Name with name of Circle
  if community matches-any CS-ALL-T5 and not community matches-any (1511:9999) then
    drop
  elseif community matches-any CS-ALL-T4 and not community matches-any (1411:9999) then
    drop
  else
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy West-Bengal-O-M_DEFAULT-ROUTE
  if destination in (0.0.0.0/0) then
    set extcommunity rt (9730:71239)
    set community (no-export)
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy West-Bengal-Data_DEFAULT-ROUTE
  if destination in (0.0.0.0/0) then
    set extcommunity rt (9730:71237)
    set community (no-export)
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy West-Bengal-O-M_IPv6-DEFAULT-ROUTE
  if destination in (::/0) then
    set extcommunity rt (9730:71239)
    set community (no-export)
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy West-Bengal-Data_IPv6-DEFAULT-ROUTE
  if destination in (::/0) then
    set extcommunity rt (9730:71237)
    set community (no-export)
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy West-Bengal-Signalling_DEFAULT-ROUTE
  if destination in (0.0.0.0/0) then
    set extcommunity rt (9730:71238)
    set community (no-export)
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy RPL_CircleZ-1512:9999_DROP_REST_T5_All_T6
  #-remark Drop other circle T5 Loopbacks, Replace Circle-Name with name of Circle
  if community matches-any CS-ALL-T5 and not community matches-any (1512:9999) then
    drop
  elseif community matches-any CS-ALL-T6 then
    drop
  else
    pass
  endif
end-policy
